1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/sarba ta thA ga ta twad twa sa gra ha nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/bod skad du/de bzhin 
1b2(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi de kho na nyid bsdus pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//bam po dang po//sangs rgyas dang byang chub 
1b3(rgyud): sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang 
1b4(rgyud): mdzad thugs rje chen po de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi rdo rje'i byin gyis brlabs dang /dam tshig gi ye shes rnam par sna tshog+sa  
1b5(rgyud): kyi khyad par dang ldan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd gyi rin po che'i dbu rgyan gyis khams gsum gyi chos kyi rgyal po dbang   
1b6(rgyud): bskur ba mnga' brnyes pa/de bzhin gshegs pa  thams cad kyi thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes rnal 'byor chen   
1b7(rgyud): po'i dbang phyug/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi phyag rgya thams cad mnyam pa nyid du thugs su chud cing //
1b8(rgyud): thams cad kyi dgongs pa mdzad pa nyid kyis sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa thaMd kyi re ba yongs su   
2a1(rgyud): @#//skong ba'i phrin las mdzad pa/thugs rje che ba rnam par snang mdzad dus gsum du rtag par bzhugs shing /sku dang gsung dang thugs thams cad rdo rje lta bur gyur pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa/de bzhin 
2a2(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad bzhugs shing bsngags bstod pa/nor bu dang /rin po che chen pos spras pa/kha dog sna tshogs shing dril bu btags pa/dar dang /'phan dang /me tog gi phreng ba dang /rnga ma dang /dra ba dang /
2a3(rgyud): dra ba phyed pa dang /zla ba rlung gis bskyod pa rnams kyis mdzes par byas pa'i 'og min gyi lha'i rgyal po'i gnas na bzhugs pa byang chub sems dpa' bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa dgu la 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' 
2a4(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rje dang /kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po dang /nam mkha'i snying po dang /rdo rje khu tshur dang /'jam dpal dang /sems bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /nam 
2a5(rgyud): mkha'i mdzod dang /bdud kyi stobs thams cad rab tu 'joms pa la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa'i sems dpa' chen po bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa dbu dang /gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 
2a6(rgyud): dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs te/de yang 'di ltar 'dzam bu'i gling na de bzhin gshegs pa tshad med grangs med pa de dag gi skus kyang til gyi gang bu bzhin du gang bar snang ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku re re las 
2a7(rgyud): kyang sangs rgyas kyi zhing grangs med tshad med pa rnams snang ngo //sangs rgyas kyi zhing de dag na yang chos kyi tshul 'di dag 'chad do//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad chen po/nam mkha'i dbyings thams 
2a8(rgyud): cad bzhin du rtag par bzhugs shing sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje lta bur gyur pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid du 'jug pas rdo rje dbyings thams cad thugs su chud pa'i ye 
2b1(rgyud): shes sems dpa' nam mkha'i dbyings thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo rdo rje'i byin gyis brlabs las byung ba'i ye shes kyi snying po/de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas pa nyid kyi rdo rje chen po'i ye shes rin po che'i 
2b2(rgyud): dbang bskur ba'i rin po che chen po/nam mkha'i dbyings thams can du spros pa'i de bzhin nyid kyi ye shes mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pas/byang chub par gyur cing /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid 
2b3(rgyud): dag pa'i phyir chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin dag par thugs su chud pa/nam mkha'i khams thams cad du khyab pa'i gzugs thams cad du yang dag par ston pa mkhyen pas sems can gyi khams ma lus shing 
2b4(rgyud): lus pa med pa'i khams rnams gdul bar spyod pa'i mchog tu gyur cing /de bzhin gshes pa thams cad kyi bka' nod cing don yod par mdzad pa nyid kyis thams cad dang mnyam pa med cing bla na med pa'i 
2b5(rgyud): phrin las thams cad mdzad pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi byang chub chen por sems brtan pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas pa thams cad spyan drangs pa'i dam tshig dang /de bzhin gshegs 
2b6(rgyud): pa thams cad mnyes par bya ba'i ye shes kyi dbang phyug dang /de bzhin gshegasapa thams cad kyis legs so zhes bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi rin po che'i dbang chen po bskur ba dang /de bzhin 
2b7(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi nyi ma'i 'od kyi dkyil 'khor dang /de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi bsam pa'i rgyal po yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i rgyal po'i rin po che'i dpal dang /de bzhin gshegs ma thams 
2b8(rgyud): cad kyi bzhad pa chen po dang /de bzhin gshegs dang /de bzhin gshegs na thams cad kyi dag pa'i chos chen po dang /de bzhin du gshegs pa thams cad kyi shes rab chen po'i ye shes dang /de bzhin gsheg pa thams cad kyi 'khor 
3a1(rgyud): @#//
3b1(rgyud): rgyas thugs//byang chub kun dang bla na med//rnam par snang mdzad rgyal ba'i gtso//rang byung gzungs dang dran pa dang //sems dpa' che dang phyag rgya che//ting 'dzin sangs rgyas phrin las mdzad//yang dag sangs 
3b2(rgyud): rgyas kun bdag nyid//yod pa rtag don rtogs mdzad pa//sdong po chen po nag po che//'dod chags chen po bde ba che//thabs chen dang ni mchog chen mchog/kun gyi mchog dang gnas dbang po//bcom 
3b3(rgyud): ldan byang chub chen po'i thugs//byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs la gnas so////de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyis//sangs 
3b4(rgyud): rgyas kyi zhing 'di dper na til gyi gang bu bzhin du yongs su gang bar gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs thams cad 'dus te byang chub sems dpa' chen pa don thams cad grub pa zhes bya ba byang chub kyi snying 
3b5(rgyud): por bzhugs pa gang na ba der gshegs te/byang chub sems dpa' de la ched du rdzogs par longs spyod pa'i sku bstan nas 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu khyod kyis gang gi phyir de bzhin gshegs 
3b6(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi kho na nyid mngon par ma rtogs par dka' ba spyod pa thams cad la spro ba bskyed cing /ji ltar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par bya snyam/de nas byang chub sems 
3b7(rgyud): dpa' sems dpa' chen po don thams cad grub pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bskul bas mi g.yo ba'i ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs te/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad la phyag 'tshal nas 
3b8(rgyud): 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de kho na nyid ni ji lta bu lags/ji ltar na bsgrub par bgyi bka' stsal par gsol/de skad ces gsol pa dang /de bzhin gshegs 
4a1(rgyud): pa de dag thams cad kyis gsung gcig tu 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu rang gi sems la rtog cing mnyam par zhog la/rang bzhin gyis grub pa'i sngags 'di ci dgar zlos shing /bsgrub par 
4a2(rgyud): gyis shig/oM tsi ta ta pra ti be d+hi ka ro mi/de nas byang chub sems dpa' don thams cad grub pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams bdag 
4a3(rgyud): gi snying ka na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor lta bur gda' ba bdag gis rtogs so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu sems de ni rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal bas na de ni ji ltar sbyangs pa de ltar 
4a4(rgyud): gyur te/dper na ras dkar po mtshon gyis bsgyur ba bzhin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyis rang bzhin gyis 'od gsalaba'i sems shes pa rgyas par bya ba'i phyir/oM bo d+hi tsit+ta ud pa ta ya 
4a5(rgyud): mi zhes bya ba rang bzhin gyis grub pa'i sngas 'dis/yang byang chub sems dpa' chen po de'i sems bskyed par gyur to//de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' des de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyis bkas byang chub kyi 
4a6(rgyud): sems bskyed nas 'di skad ces gsol to/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor lta bu mthong ba gang lags pa de ni zla ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid du mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa tha mas cad kyis bka' stsal pa/de bzhin 
4a7(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po sems bskyed pa kun tu bzang po khyod kyis mngon sum du gyur gyis/de legs par bsgrubs shig/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sems bskyed pa kun tu bzung po 
4a8(rgyud): de brtan par bya ba'i phyir sngags 'dis rang gi snying gar zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la rdo rje'i dbyibs su soms shig/oM tiSh+Tha badzra/byang chub sems dpas gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 
4b1(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng du rdo rje 'dug par bdag gis mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bka' stsal pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi kun tu bzang po'i sems rdo rje 'di sngags 'dis
4b2(rgyud): brtan par gyis shig/oM badzra Ang ma konya ha~M//de nas nam mkha'i dbyings thams cad du mnyam par 'jug pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje dbyings ji snyed pa de dag thams cad
4b3(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams  cad kyi byin brlabs kyis sems dpa'i rdo rje der bzhugs so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyis bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' chen po don thams cad grub
4b4(rgyud): pa de la rdo rje'i dbyings /rdo rje'i dbyings zhes bya ba'i ming gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje dbyings kyis/de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das
4b5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa rnams /de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi d+ha rma'i sku ni bdag legs par mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bka' stsal pa//sems dpa' chen po de lta bas na/sems
4b6(rgyud): dpa'i rdo rje de rnam pa'i mchog thams cad dang ldan pa'i sangs rgyas kyi skur bdag nyid rang bzhin gyis grub pa rig sngags ci dga' bar zlos la sgoms shig/oM ya tha sarba ta thA ga ta sta tha a haM//de nas de skad ces
4b7(rgyud): bka' stsal pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' chenapo rdo rje'i dbyings kyis bdag nyid de bzhin gshegs pa'i mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thAms cad la phyag byas te 'di skad ces
4b8(rgyud): gsol to//bcom ldan 'da'as de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis bdag byin gyis rlobs la mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa 'di brtan par mdzod cig/de nas de skad ces gsol pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa
5a1(rgyud): @#//thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdo rje dbyings kyi sems dpa'i rdo rje ngar zhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje'i dbyings kyis skad cig ma de nyid kyi tshe na/de bzhin gshegs 
5a2(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes kyi phyag rgya gsang ba'i dam tshig du zhugs so//de bzhin gshegs
5a3(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi rdo rje rin po che'i dbang bskur ba mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes gyi phyag rgya gsang ba'i dam tshigi du zhugs so//de bzhin bshegs pa thams cad kyi chos mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes rtogs pa rang bzhin gyis
5a4(rgyud): dag go/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi thams cad mnyam pa nyid rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal zhing ye shes kyi 'byung gnas su gyur pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i 
5a5(rgyud): sangs rgyas su gyur to//de nas yang de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi sems dpa'i rdo rje de las de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams bcad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes
5a6(rgyud): bskyed/nam mkha' las byung ba'i nor bu rin po che chen po'i dbang bskur bas dbang bskur ro//kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po'i chos kyi ye shes bskyed/de bzhin gshegs pa thamag cad kyi phrin las 
5a7(rgyud): thams cad la bzhag nas/ri rab kyi rtse mo gang na ba dang /khang bu brtsegs pa rtse mo rdo rje nor bu dang rin po che las byas pa gang na ba der gshegs te byon nas/de bzhin gshegs rdo rje dbyings de bzhin 
5a8(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad du byin gyis brlabs nas dbyer med par gyur te/de bzhin gshegs pa tha mas cad kyi seng ge'i gdan la zhal kun tu bstan te bzhugs par gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs 
5b1(rgyud): pa mi bskyod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rin chen 'byung ldan dang /de bzhin gshegs spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po'i rgyal po dang /de bzhin gshegs ba gdon mi za bar grub pa rnams/de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
5b2(rgyud): cad du so so nas bdag nyid kyis byin gyis brlabs te/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyam pa nyid du rab tu rtogs pa'i phyir phyogs thams cad mnyam pa nyid tu dmigs nas phyogs 
5b3(rgyud): bzhir bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad de bzhin gshegs pa mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas ring po ma lon pa de bzhin gshegs pa thabs cad kyi thugs kun tu bzang po zhes 
5b4(rgyud): bya ba/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi nam mkha' las byung ba'i nor bu rin po che chen po'i dbang gis dbang bskur ba/de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas cad kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbangapo'i chos kyi ye shes dam 
5b5(rgyud): pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa brnyes pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tha mas cad kyi phrin las thams cad gdon mi za bar ston pa/thogs pa med pa/dgongs pa rdzogs pa/yid kyi bsam pa'o cog yongs su rdzogs pas de bzhin 
5b6(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad du bdag nyid kyis byin gyis brlabs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po zhes bya ba'i dam tshig las byung ba/sems dpa'i byin gyis 
5b7(rgyud): brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegasapa thams cad kyi theg pa chen po mngon par rtogs pa zhes bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs 
5b8(rgyud): ka nas phyung ngo //badzra satwa/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi thugs ka nas byung ma thag pa 'di ni/bcom ldan 'das kun tu bzang po de nyid zla ba'i dkyil 'khor du gyur nas byung ste/sems can thams 
6a1(rgyud): @#//
6b1(rgyud): pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba nas/ji srid sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams yongs su skyob cing thams cad la phan pa dang /bde ba mdzad pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes 
6b2(rgyud): dang mngon par shes pa dam pa'i dngos grub bsgrub pa la sogs pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams kyi bar du bstan nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni kun tu bzang po yin pa dang shin tu 
6b3(rgyud): brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po'i skur gyur te/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas/'di skad du ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//
6b4(rgyud): e ma'o bdag ni rang byung ba'i//kun tu bzang po sems dpa' brtan//de ni brtan phyir lus min yang //sems dpa'i lus su gyur pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen mo kun tu bzang po'i sku bcom ldan 'das kyi 
6b5(rgyud): thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi spyan snga na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas/bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ting nge 'dzin dang /
6b6(rgyud): ye shes kyi dam tshig rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhags nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi tshul khrims dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /shes rab dang /rnam par grol ba dang /rnam par grol pa'i ye shes mthong ba dang /chos 
6b7(rgyud): kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang sems can gyi don bya ba dang /thabs chen po dang /stobs dang /brtson 'grus dang /ye shes chen po'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i rnams yongs su bskyab 
6b8(rgyud): pa dang /thams cad kyi dbang por bya ba dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang /yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs par thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes dang /mngon par shes pa bla na med pa dang /theg pa chen po 
7a1(rgyud): @#//
7b1(rgyud): rnams byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad spyan drang ba la sogs pa sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu gdon mi za ba'i rgyal po yin 
7b2(rgyud): pa dang /shin tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gdon mi za ba'i lus su gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas te ched du brjod pa 
7b3(rgyud): 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o rdo rje las byung ba'i//lcags kyu nges pa'i rgyal po bdag/gang phyir kun tu sangs rgyas khyab//grub phyir kun nas spyan 'dren to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po shin tu gdon 
7b4(rgyud): mi za ba'i rgyal po'i sku bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gsheg pa thams cad kyi g.yas phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de 
7b5(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad spyan drang ba'i dam tshig gi rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams can spyan drang ba'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa 
7b6(rgyud): med pa thams cad dgug pa dang thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad 'du ba'i byin gyis brlabs dam pa'i bar du bsgrub pa'i phyir rdo rje'i lcags kyu de byang chub sems dpa' 
7b7(rgyud): gdon mi za ba'i rgyal po de'i lag par de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis rdo rje dgug pa rdo rje dgu pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gis dbang skur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje dgug pa 
7b8(rgyud): rdo rje lcags kyu des de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad spyan 'dren cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi rdo rje yi//ye shes bla na med pa 'di//sangs rgyas kun gyi don grub phyir//kun nas spyan drang 
8a1(rgyud): @#//
8b1(rgyud): cad mnyes par byed pa'i byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdud rdo rje dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa 
8b2(rgyud): rnams mgu par bya ba dang /thams cad kyis bde ba dang /yid bde ba myong bar byas nas/de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi bdud kyi mchog 'grub par 'thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir rdo rje'i mda' de byang chub 
8b3(rgyud): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bdud ces bya ba de'i phyag tu de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis rdo rje'i gzhu rdo rje gzhu zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa'
8b4(rgyud): chen po rdo rje'i gzhu zhes bya bas rdo rje'i mda' des de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad la bdud byed cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi 'dod chags kyi//ye shes 'di ni dri ma med//'dod chags 
8b5(rgyud): kyis ni chags bral bcom//bde ba thams cad sbyin par byed//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis yang byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po'i dam tshig las byung ba'i sems dpa'i byin 
8b6(rgyud): gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rab tu dga' ba'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas 
8b7(rgyud): byung ngo //badzra sA d+hu//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de legs so zhes bya bar gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi 
8b8(rgyud): thugs su zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyibs su gyur te phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyabs de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang 
9a1(rgyud): @#//mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis legs so zhes bya ba la sogs pa dang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/
9a2(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu dga' bar byed pa nyid dang /rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po'i lus su gyur te/bcom ldan 
9a3(rgyud): 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas par gyur nas ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o thams cad mkhyen kun gyi//legs sbyin nang na bdag mchog bas//rnam rtog bral ba de 
9a4(rgyud): dag gi/nges par bgyes pa bskyed pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po rab du dga' ba'i rgyal po'i sku de bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs gnas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi rgyab na zla ba'i dkyil 
9a5(rgyud): 'khor la gnas nas/yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyes par byed pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin 
9a6(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi rab tu dgyes pa bla na med pa'i ye shes kyi dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i sems can thams cad mgu bar bya ba dang thams cad bde ba dang yid bde ba myong 
9a7(rgyud): bar bya ba nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi dgyes pa'i ro bla na med pa dam pa 'grub pa 'thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir rdo rje dgyes pa de byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rab tu dga' ba'i 
9a8(rgyud): rgyal po de'i lag tu de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas cad kyis rdo rje dgyes pa rdo rje dgyes pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po rdo rje 
9b1(rgyud): dgyes pas rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyibs des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la legs so zhes bya bas rab tu dgyes par byed cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi legs sbyin pa//'di ni rab tu 
9b2(rgyud): 'jug byed pas//rdo rje thams cad dgyes par byed//lha rdzas dgyes pa spel ba'o//byang chub kyi sems chen po dang de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad spyan drang ba'i dam tshig dang de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas cad 
9b3(rgyud): mnyes par byed pa'i ye shes dang /dgyes pa chen po dang 'di bzhin de bzhin gshegs pa thams/cad kyi dam tshig chen po'i sems dpa'o//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
9b4(rgyud): chen po nam mkha'i snying po'i dam tshig las byung ba/rin po che'i byin gyis rlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis dbang bskur ba'i dam tshig 
9b5(rgyud): ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo //badzra rad na/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams bcad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 
9b6(rgyud): 'dzin/thams cad nam mkha' dang mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes shin tu rtogs pa yin pa'i phyir/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de nam mkha' thams cad kyi 'od zer du gyur nas byung ngo //nam mkha' thams cad kyi 
9b7(rgyud): 'od zer de dag gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad snang bar byas pas thams cad nam mkha'i dbyings lta bur gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i thams cad kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis/nam mkha'i dbyings de dag thams cad 
9b8(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin shin tu bsgoms par gyur pa'i phyir yang nam mkha'i khams thams cad las byung ba'i/'jig rten gyi khams thams
10a1(rgyud): @#//cad du mnyam par 'jug pa'i tshad tsam gyi rdo rje rin po che chen po'i dbyibs su gyur nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje rin po che'i dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi 
10a2(rgyud): rdul phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi skur gyur nas de bzhin gshegasapa thams cad kyi dbang bskur ba la sogs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams 'jig 
10a3(rgyud): rten gyi khams thams cad du byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni nam mkha'i khams thams cad kyi snying po las shin tu byung ba dang /rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' 
10a4(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po nam mkha'i snying po'i skur gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o chags med rgyal ba yang //khams gsum dbang por 
10a5(rgyud): dgongs pas na//rdo rje rin chen dam pa yi//dbang gi mchog ni bdag yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po nam ma mkha'i snying po'i lus de bcom ldan 'das kyis thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin 
10a6(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyan snga nas zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi nor bu rin po che rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 
10a7(rgyud): 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi dgongs pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i don thams cad yongs su grub par 
10a8(rgyud): bya ba dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs par thams cad kyi don dam pa phun sum tshogs pa grub pa thob pa'i phyir//byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po nam mkha'i 
10b1(rgyud): snying po rdo rje rin po che'i 'khor lo bskor bar rdo rje rin po che'i myu gu'i dbang gis dbang bskur nas lag tu rdo rje'i nor bu de byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis rdo rje snying po rdo rje zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gis dbang 
10b2(rgyud): bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje'i snying pos rdo rje'i nor bu de rang gis dbang bskur ba'i gnas su 'jog cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi sems dpa'i dbyibs 
10b3(rgyud): dbang bskur de ni bdag yin te/bdag gi lag tu byin pas na//rin chen la ni rin chen bzhag/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' ses dpa' chen po gzi brjid chen po zhes bya ba'i dam 
10b4(rgyud): tshig las byung ba'i rin po che'i byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi 'od zer gyi dam tshig ces bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi 
10b5(rgyud): snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo //badzra te dza//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de nyid de/nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen por gyur nas/
10b6(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje nyi ma'i dbyibs su gyur te/bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor de 
10b7(rgyud): las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sku byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa/thams cad kyi 'od zer 'gyed pa la sogs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs 
10b8(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu gzi brjid che ba nyid dang rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur na/byang chub sems dpa' sems

11b5(rgyud): mtshan bsgreng ba la sogs pa sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni/rin po che'i dpal dang shin tu brtan pa yin pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur pas/byang 
11b6(rgyud): chub sems dpa' rin po che dpal gyi skur gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rna bar snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o don kun grub pa dang //dgongs pa thams cad rdzogs pa yi//
11b7(rgyud): don rnams thams cad rdzogs byed pa'i//'dri med dpal ni bdag yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rin po che'i dpal gyi sku bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa 
11b8(rgyud): thams cad kyi g.yon phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing 'dug par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rgyal mtshan gyi byin gyi brlabs rdo rje zhes bya 
12a1(rgyud): ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi nor bu yid bzhin gyi rgyal po'i rgyal mtshan bsgreng ba'i dam tshig dang sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa 
12a2(rgyud): rnams kyi re ba thams cad yongs su bskang bar bya ba dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang /yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i thams cad kyi don chen po dngos grub dam pa thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag 
12a3(rgyud): gi phyir rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan de byang chub sems dpa' chen po rin po che'i dpal de'i lag tu de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan/rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan zhes/rdo rje'i ming gis dbang

12a5(rgyud): brjod pa'i de chad du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa kun//yongs su rdzogs par byed pa yi//yid bzhin nor bu'i rgyal mtshan ni//sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin pa'i tshul//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis 
12a6(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po rtag par dga' zhing rab tu mgu ba'i dbang po'i dam tshig las byung ba rin po che'i byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin 
12a7(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad dgyes pa'i dam tshig ces bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo //badzra has/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tha mas cad kyi thugs las byung ma 
12a8(rgyud): theg pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid do//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi 'dzum par gyur te byung nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/

12b2(rgyud): nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rmad du byung ba la sogs pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni rtag tu dga' zhing rab tu mgu ba'i 
12b3(rgyud): dbang po yin pa dang shin tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rtag tu dga' zhing rab du mgu ba'i dbang po'i lus su gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad 
12b4(rgyud): kyi thugs la gnas te ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//rtag tu mnyam par bzhag rnams kyis//sangs rgyas don la rab sbyor phyir//e ma'o bdag ni kun mchog gi/bzhad chen rmad du byung ba yin//de nas byang 
12b5(rgyud): chub sems dpa' sems dpa'i chen po rtag par dga' zhing rab tu mgu pa'i dbang po'i lus bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rgyab kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas 
12b6(rgyud): yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rmad du byung ba byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs 
12b7(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi rmad du byung ba bskyed pa'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa thams cad kyi dbang po bla na med pa bya ba dang thams cad bde ba dang /yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs 
13a1(rgyud): @#//
13b1(rgyud): de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin du phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ting nge 'dzin dang /ye shes dang mngon par shes pa 
13b2(rgyud): la sogs pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang /cho 'phrul rnams 'jig rten gyi khams tham cad khyab par byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po yin pa dang 
13b3(rgyud): shin tu brtan pa yin pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun du spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang bo'i bskur gyur te/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas 
13b4(rgyud): ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o rang byung gdod nas dag/dam pa'i don ni bdag yin te/gang phyir gzings 'dra'i chos rnams las//rnam par dag pa dmigs par gyur//de nas byang chub sems dpa'

13b6(rgyud): bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi ting nge 'dzin mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes dam tshig las byung ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin 
13b7(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad rnam par sbyang ba'i dam tshig dang sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams kyi bdag dang gzhan rnam par dag par bya ba dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang /yid bde ba myong bar bya ba

14b3(rgyud): chub sems dpa' sems dpal chen po 'jam dpal gyi sku de bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi g.yas phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod

14b5(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa thams cad gcad pa'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams kyi sdug bsngal thams cad gcad pa dang thams cad kyi bde ba dang /
14b6(rgyud): yid bde ba rnams myong bar byas nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi gsung gi rjes su 'brang ba'i shes rab dam pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir rdo rje ral gri de byang chub 
14b7(rgyud): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'jam dpal de'i lag tu de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis rdo rje blo rdo rje blo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//
14b8(rgyud): de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che na po rdo rje blos de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la rdo rje ral gri des 'debs shing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//shes 
15a1(rgyud): rab pha rol phyin pa'i tshul//dgra rnams thams cad tshar gcod cing //sdig pa thams cad 'joms pa'i mchog/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sems 
15a2(rgyud): bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i dam tshig las byung ba'i chos kyi byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor lo'i dam tshig 
15a3(rgyud): ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo //badzra he tu/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de 
15a4(rgyud): nyid de/rdo rje dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor chen po la sogs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi dkyil 'khor du gyur te byung nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te gcig tu 'dres par 
15a5(rgyud): gyur nas/rdo rje 'khor lo'i dbyibs su gyur te bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag gnyis su gnas par gyur to//de nas rdo rje 'khor lo'i dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i 
15a6(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cid kyi sku rnams byung nas/sems bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba la sogs pa sangs rgyas kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams 'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad du byas 
15a7(rgyud): te/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni sems bskyed ma thag tu chosakyi 'khor lo bskor ba yin pa dang shin tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sems bskyed 
15a8(rgyud): ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor la bskor ba zhes bya ba'i skur gyur te/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas te ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//rdo rje dam pa chos can gyi//e ma'o rdo rje 'khor lo 
15b1(rgyud): bdag/gang phyir sems bskyed tsam gyis kyang //chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bar 'gyur//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sems bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i sku de bcom ldan 
15b2(rgyud): 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi g.yon phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs

123b3(rgyud): ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi dngos grub kyi rgyud de/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor 'od bcas pa'i//phyag rgya chen po bcings nas ni//rang gi lus la ji ltar shes//de ltar 'gro ba'i yid shes so//de la 'di ni rdo rje'i rigs 
123b4(rgyud): kyi ye shes kyi dngos grub kyi rgyud do//nam mkha' 'am ni phyogs gzhan du//'phos par gyur nas dkyil 'khor ni//ji lta bur yang mthong 'gyur la//de lta bur ni yid shes so//de la 'dir ni pad mo'i rigs kyi ye shes 
123b5(rgyud): kyi dngos grub kyi rgyud do////nam mkha' 'am sa phyogs phra mo'am//yi ge phreng ba mthong 'gyur na//mdor na kha dog ci 'dra bar//shes pa de 'drar des sems shes//de la 'di ni nor bu'i rigs kyi ye shes kyi dngos grub gyi rgyud do//

151a3(rgyud): rnam par rgyal ba'i dngos grub dang /dbang thams cad kyi dngos grub dang /bde ba dang yid bde ba thams cad kyi dngos grub pa dang /dngos grub dam pa rnams so//de la 'di ni 'jig rten gsum las rnam par rgyal ba'i 
151a4(rgyud): dngos grub yin te/khams gsum rnam par rgyal ba'i phyag rgya bcings la dbang phyug chen po rkang pa g.yon pas mnan te bsgrub par bya'o//de nas gzugs de skad 'byin pa dang hU~M zhes bya ba brjod par bya'o//
151a5(rgyud): hU~M zhes brjod pa tsam gyis dbang phyug chen po la sogs pa'i jig rten gsum po thams cad kyi bdag po 'khor dang bcas pa rnams sgrub pa'i mdun du bka' nyan cing dbang du 'gyur ro//de phyin chad 'jig rten 
151a6(rgyud): gsum po thams cad kyi bdang po rdo rje 'dzin par 'gyur te nam mkha' la 'gro zhing 'jig rten gsum po 'khor lo thams cad kun tu bskor te slar 'ongs nas lha mo rungs pa la sogs pa sems can thams 
151a7(rgyud): cad hU~M zhes bya bas 'dul bar byed do//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje hU~M zhes bya ba'i gzugs dang ldan pas 'jig rten gsum po thams can la bka' stsol cing rdo rje sems dpar bcas pa'i de bzhin 
151a8(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad mthong nas mnyes par byed cing lo stong du 'tsho bar byed par bcom ldan 'das rdo rje hU~M zhes byed pas gsungs so//de la 'di ni dbang thams cad kyi dngos grub ste/phyag rgya bsgrub pa na de

151b2(rgyud): che'i dbang dang /chos kyi dbang dang /las kyi dbang rnams so//de la rdo rje'i dbang thob na de bzhin gshegs pa thams cadakyi rdo rje 'dzin par 'gyur ro//rin po che'i dbang thob na rin po che thams cad kyi bdag por 
151b3(rgyud): 'gyur ro//chos kyi dbang gis ni chos thams cad kyi rgyal por 'gyur ro//las kyi dbang gis ni 'jig rten las 'das pa'i las thams cad kyi dngos grub thob par 'gyur ro zhes bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs 
151b4(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi dbang gis gsungs so//de la 'di ni bde ba dang yid bde ba thams cad kyi dngos grub ste/de yang 'di ltar rtag tu gsang ba'i mchod pa rnams kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad la mchod pa byas 
151b5(rgyud): la/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba mtha' yas cad kyi dngos grub thob bar 'gyur ro//zhes bcom ldan 'das de bzhIn gshegs pa thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bdeb thams cad kyi gsungs 
151b6(rgyud): so//de la 'di ni dngos grub dam pa ste/de yang ji ltar rdo rje 'dzin pa dang bdag mnyam mo zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pas gsungs so//de la 'd-i dag ni pad mo rigs kyi dngos grub rnams te/de ni 'di ltar mnyes 
151b7(rgyud): par bya ba dang /dbang du bya ba dang /bsrung ba dang /pad mo'i dngos grub rnams so//de la mnyes par bya ba'i dngos grub ni 'di yin te/ji ltar pad mo 'dod pa bsgoms na de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad
151b8(rgyud): la sogs pa sems can thams cad mnyes par byed nus par 'gyur ro//bdag nyid 'jig rten dbang phyug rjes su dran par byas na sems can thams cad dbang du byed nus par 'gyur ro/byams pas

152b7(rgyud): bsod nams chen po thob 'gyur te/bsod nams med kyang myur bar ni chos sbyin pas na dngos grub thob//de la 'di ni rig pa'i bsod nams kyi dngos bsgrub kyi rgyud de/bsod nams nang na sbyin pa
